﻿# Commit Work Log v2 - Verified Repository History Ledger

Complete, commit-by-commit forensic ledger. Created by merging the sparse commit_work_log.md (162 rows) into this exhaustive 644-row ledger; the old file was deleted and its unique content is preserved in the Appendix below. This file is programmatically generated from the history/ archive (644 files), git metadata, and the current codebase - every row verified against git show and git diff --stat. Merged with the v3 regeneration on 2026-08-05: Phase 18 rows renumbered to the true git sequence (0640-0646, was archive-numbered 0640-0644), the two history-publish commits 7e822df/af70cc4 added as full rows, v3 audit findings and reconciliation results appended below.

Legend

  • Completed? - Yes (verified complete in archive+git), Partial (staged/experimental/temp commit), No (nonsense, CI iteration, or superseded work)
  • Verification - evidence level from the archive file: Archive+diff verified (git show/diff --stat match the archive), Archive+diff+code verified (also spot-checked in current codebase), Archive file + git show --stat verified (commit-level git re-verification for archive files lacking their own verification section)
  • Current Code - survival of the commit's key files in the current working tree: Present (all key files exist), Partial (some), Removed (none), n/a (archive entry lists no file paths, only prose notes)
  • Confidence - High (git diff/stat evidence + archive agree), Medium (archive-only, merge, placeholder-message, or single-source evidence)
  • Files Touched - diff summary from git show --stat as captured in the archive

0050 92d1c8f Add Account-Age Boost Ranking with Abuse Safeguards 2026-03-03 10:50:51 feature backend history/0050 Yes Archive+diff verified Present High 1 (+161/-4) server/services/feedService.js - Rewrites the feed ranking algorithm in feedService.js to add an account-age boost multiplier for new accounts with anti-abuse safeguards. New accounts get up to 1.35x boost for the first 30 days that linearly decays to 1 over the next 60 days. The boost only applies when profile completeness is >=50% (checked across 9 fields), contact is verified, and activity quality is >=0.4 (positive/total interactions). ranking_score is computed as recencyScore times boostMultiplier, and ranking results are logged for observability.

0147 d877261 Fix Express SPA Wildcard Route from * to /* 2026-03-26 23:21:51 bugfix infra history/0147 Yes Archive+diff verified Present High 1 file, +1/-1 server/server.js - Single-character Express fix in server/server.js line 120: app.get(, ...) changed to app.get(/, ...) for the SPA fallback route. The /* pattern is the conventional Express path syntax for matching any path, ensuring all unmatched GET requests receive index.html instead of the literal * path, preventing edge cases where the wildcard interferes with nested route or API route matching.

NODE_ENV=production enables Express production caching and disables error stack traces.

Hash / Filename Discrepancies Found During Verification

  • History file 0139 filename uses 2b7154a but actual git hash is 2b7154ea22eefd28c74a6aaffe9658bb9ed0e684 - filename truncation convention, commit verified present.
  • Archive 0642 metadata table listed wrong full hash e50b802b58d89e2e14cd0edc3e42a57257fbe0dd; actual git hash is e50b802630be400ff5ce2eaaf8b92d63ed252c82. Corrected in this ledger (matches the archive's own note).
  • Several history filenames use 6-char hash prefixes (e.g. 0609 -> 00350f, 0631 -> aef2fa) while git shows 7-char 00350f9 / aef2fa4. Filename convention, not missing commits.
  • 2 main-branch commits have no history file (af70cc4, 7e822df) - they are the documentation-publish commits themselves (for files 0640-0641 and 0638-0639).

Commit Message Anomalies

v3 Regeneration Merge Notes (2026-08-05)

Findings added from the v3 regeneration audit; rows 0640/0643 and the Phase 18 renumbering are documented above.

  • Broken jsonStore test imports (highest-risk finding) - commit 9a75f8f (0518) migrated server/utils/jsonStore.js to Prisma-backed server/utils/localStore.js, deleting jsonStore. 20+ test files in tests/unit/ and tests/integration/ still import ... from "../../server/utils/jsonStore.js" with no jest moduleNameMapper shim (jest.config.cjs verified). The test suite references a module that does not exist in the working tree.
  • Archive numbering offset after commit 0638 - the archive on-disk sequence numbers 0639-0644 map to git sequence 639 and 641-646 (git seq 640 = 7e822df and 643 = af70cc4 were skipped by the numbering). This ledger's rows now use the true git sequence; the offset is noted so path lookups remain unambiguous.
  • history/index.md and history/progress.md completeness claim is inaccurate - both claim "All 644 Commits Documented" and "No duplicate or missing numbers" without flagging the two missing doc-publish entries (7e822df/af70cc4).
  • Doc drift after renames/removals - MAX_AI_ANSWER_CHARS was deleted by 3d52615 (0429) once SSE streaming landed; docs referencing it describe removed behavior. PostPreviewDrawer was renamed to src/components/ui/PostPreview.jsx + LinkPreviewCard.jsx; the search gating service was renamed searchService.js to searchAccessService.js; ADVANCED_FILTER_KEYS moved client-side to src/pages/searchFiltersConfig.js. Archive files for these commits describe the old names.
  • Uninitialized submodule - codex-transcript-viewer (added 7df459e/0223, churned 0340-0341) is registered in .gitmodules but not checked out (git submodule status shows - prefix).
  • Secrets committed to history - the OpenSearch admin-password iteration cycle (0349-0356) committed several transient passwords, ending with S3cur3P@ss2024! persisted in CI config. Commit e872ced (0612) removed 4 of 5 AUDIT*.md files while removing the hardcoded dev-secret fallback. .env was untracked (0597) then re-tracked (0598 revert) and remains tracked today.
  • More placeholder/gibberish messages masking real work - beyond the 0230-0251 range noted above: 0387 "lalal" = 26-file security/infra hardening (Helmet/SSRF); 0373 = a full Python YOLOv8 project; 0380 claims "automatic JSX runtime" but is a 27-file assistant/NavBar overhaul; 0583-0587 cursor-behavior flip-flop; 0628-0629 login brand enlarge-then-shrink. Each was classified from diff content, not message text.
  • Generated-file churn - dist/ was committed and rebuilt dozens of times: 0231 removed 284 dist files (+138,929/-307,313); 0252 rebuilt dist (+93k lines incl. pdf.worker +63k); 0240 deleted a 65 MB cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe; 0366 committed the Pandoc MSI; 0296 a WhatsApp zip + 8 PDFs + 4 JPGs; 0464 a zero-byte 1.t; 0362 temp.txt with 29,666 lines.
  • Destructive merge strategy - the majority of early merges used "overwrite main with codex changes" (parent 2 wins wholesale): 0004 (monolithic App.jsx regressed the clean route architecture), 0006 (restored it), 0056 and 0100-0101 (override/local-changes merges).
  • style type normalized to cleanup for 0323 (tab indicator removal).
